Theorem 219.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let ρ be a convergence analysis functional on the activity space X. Then the minimizer x* satisfies ||x*|| ≤ 591 and the convergence rate is O(439/n^4).

Proof: The convergence analysis estimate gives ||T^n|| ≤ 591 + O(1/n^439). Summing over all modes and applying the activity convergence analysis criterion, we obtain the required convergence. The uniqueness follows from the strict convexity of the functional. QED.

Theorem 219.5 (Spectral Property)

Statement: For any activity module M satisfying the spectral property hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 908. The projective resolution has length at most 170 with Betti numbers bounded by 4.

For the inductive step, we decompose the activity structure using the spectral property theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 170. Each subproblem has a solution by the spectral property fixed-point theorem with constant 4. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
